Multi-team isolation via per-team folder names.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003cstrong\\u003eQ: What happens when a new Claude / OpenAI / Google model ships? Do I need to update the plugin?\\u003c/strong\\u003e\\nNo — the plugin uses a shared model curator (\\u003ccode\\u003escripts/resolve_model.py\\u003c/code\\u003e) that resolves aliases (\\u003ccode\\u003elatest-balanced-anthropic\\u003c/code\\u003e, \\u003ccode\\u003elatest-text-google\\u003c/code\\u003e, etc.) at call time. When a model is deprecated, the curator auto-falls-forward to the replacement. \\u003ccode\\u003e/digital-marketing-pro:doctor\\u003c/code\\u003e reports registry age and severity (\\u003ccode\\u003eok\\u003c/code\\u003e \\u0026lt;60d / \\u003ccode\\u003ewarn\\u003c/code\\u003e 60-119d / \\u003ccode\\u003eurgent\\u003c/code\\u003e ≥120d); when stale it prints the exact \\u003ccode\\u003epython scripts/refresh_models.py\\u003c/code\\u003e invocation to poll the provider APIs for drift. Plus \\u003ccode\\u003esettings.json.example\\u003c/code\\u003e ships a \\u003ccode\\u003efallbackModel\\u003c/code\\u003e chain so Claude Code transparently swaps models on overload.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003cstrong\\u003eQ: Is this an Anthropic product?\\u003c/strong\\u003e\\nNo — independent open-source plugin built by \\u003ca href=\\\"https://indranil.in\\\" rel=\\\"nofollow\\\"\\u003eIndranil Banerjee\\u003c/a\\u003e. MIT-licensed. Runs on Claude Code + Cowork.\\u003c/p\\u003e\\n\\u003cp dir=\\\"auto\\\"\\u003e\\u003cstrong\\u003eQ: I found a compliance rule that looks out of date.\\u003c/strong\\u003e\\n\\u003ca href=\\\"https://github.com/indranilbanerjee/digital-marketing-pro/issues\\\"\\u003eFile an issue\\u003c/a\\u003e with the citation. class=\"react-directory-commit-message\"><a data-pjax=\"true\" title=\"feat(v3.1.0): remove all global hooks, especially the mcp_.* gatekeeper\n\nAudit surfaced the same multi-plugin coexistence issue that prompted\nContentForge v3.9.0 and SocialForge v1.5.0 the same day. Claude Code plugin\nhooks fire globally on every operation in every project, with no per-directory\nscoping. DMP's prior 4 hooks were particularly invasive because one of them —\nPreToolUse with matcher &quot;mcp_.*&quot; — intercepted EVERY MCP tool call from EVERY\ninstalled plugin (Slack, Notion, GitHub MCP, Stripe, anything), forcing a\nbrand-compliance LLM evaluation on every MCP write regardless of context.\n\nRemoved:\n- SessionStart: ran setup.py --check-deps on every Claude Code launch\n- PreToolUse Write|Edit: brand compliance + hallucination check on every edit\n- PreToolUse mcp_.*: brand-compliance gate on every MCP call from every plugin\n  (the most acute offender for users running multiple MCP-using plugins)\n- SessionEnd: insight-saving prompt on every session end\n\nAll work preserved at the right architectural layer:\n- Compliance and hallucination checks live in the agents that produce content\n- Setup runs on demand: python3 scripts/setup.py --check-deps --summary\n- MCP-write approvals live in per-agent decision flows that already require\n  explicit user approval for marketing operations\n- Insight capture is opt-in via the engagement orchestrator\n\nPrior hook config preserved with rationale notes at\nhooks/hooks-reference.example.json. To re-enable a hook, copy its entry into\nhooks/hooks.json — but understand the global-scoping caveat.\n\nNo breaking changes to commands, skills, agents, or the v3.0 12-Part\nEngagement Methodology. Output quality and compliance are unchanged.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) &lt;noreply@anthropic.com&gt;\" class=\"Link--secondary\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<blockquote>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Your agency just signed a 50-brand client. The previous agency left no playbook. Three brands are bleeding budget, two have stale positioning, one is launching in a regulated jurisdiction next month. Where do you start?</strong></p>\n</blockquote>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Run <code>/digital-marketing-pro:engagement</code> against each brand. Same 12-Part Strategy Flow, same Four Core Documents, same 61-step structure — auditable across the entire portfolio in ~60 minutes per brand on Claude Opus-class models (measured on Opus 4.8; Opus 5 is the current equivalent at the same price). No more inconsistent depth between brands. No more \"what did the last agency do?\" mysteries. No more compliance gaps in regulated jurisdictions.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Open-source AI marketing plugin — <strong>158 skills, 24 specialist agents, EU AI Act Article 50 ready, Cowork team-persistent</strong>. Built for marketing agencies, in-house teams running 50–200 brands, and consultancies. Installs on <strong>Claude Code</strong> (CLI + IDE), <strong>Anthropic Cowork</strong>, <strong>OpenAI Codex</strong>, <strong>Cursor 2.5+</strong>, <strong>GitHub Copilot CLI</strong>, <strong>Google Antigravity 2.0</strong>, <strong>Hermes Agent</strong>, and <strong>OpenClaw</strong> + 35+ Agent Skills platforms. Created by <a href=\"https://indranil.in\" rel=\"nofollow\">Indranil Banerjee</a> · <a href=\"https://www.linkedin.com/in/askneelnow/\" rel=\"nofollow\">LinkedIn</a> · <a href=\"https://x.com/askneelnow\" rel=\"nofollow\">X</a>.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><a href=\"/indranilbanerjee/digital-marketing-pro/blob/main/CHANGELOG.md\"><img shipped — v3.17.1 (July 30, 2026): registry reconciliation + the anonymity guard.</strong> The canonical model registry caught up with the July-9 GPT-5.6 GA and the Claude 5 family on its balanced/fast aliases (<code>latest-balanced-anthropic</code> → claude-sonnet-5, <code>latest-balanced-openai</code> → gpt-5.6-terra, <code>latest-fast-openai</code> → gpt-5.6-luna; GPT-5.5/5.4 marked superseded with replacement routing) — caught by this repo's own cross-registry drift tests. Plus a new machine-enforced source-anonymity guard that scans every file on every test run. Previously — <strong>v3.17.0 (July 29): the Line-by-Line Audit.</strong> Every one of the repo's 530 files — all 158 skills, 24 agents, 18 commands, 86 scripts, and every doc — was read end-to-end by a 16-reader audit fleet and re-verified against July-2026 ground truth. ~250 fixes shipped: dead platforms and retired tools purged (Google Podcasts, Chartable, GSC legacy tools, GARM, HARO, giropay…), stale API/model claims corrected, doc↔script contract drift eliminated (every documented flag, payload, and threshold now matches the code), the storage split-brain fully closed, statistical tables regenerated from the plugin's own calculators, and the self-containment guard extended to docs/, scripts/, and root. 210/210 tests green. <a href=\"#whats-new\">Read what's new →</a> · <a href=\"/indranilbanerjee/digital-marketing-pro/blob/main/CHANGELOG.md\">Full changelog →</a></p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><sub>Previously — <strong>v3.15.0 (July 7, 2026):</strong> The <strong>Reliability &amp; Truth</strong> release — a full-repo audit fixed ~200 findings in one pass. Connectors are honestly opt-in (the shipped <code>.mcp.json</code> is empty; fictional npm packages purged), all 18 execution skills carry a uniform typed-approval gate (closes issue #6), the Tessl review workflow moved to the <code>tessl review</code> CLI (closes issue #8), agents consolidated 25 → 24, C2PA gained the Article 50 <code>--ai-disclosure</code> assertion, and a new doc-vs-argparse contract linter + state-layer tests grew the suite from 123 to 207 passing. <a href=\"#whats-new\">Read what's new →</a> · <a SSRF/DNS coverage</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>License</td>\n<td><strong>MIT — no telemetry, no seats</strong></td>\n<td>Proprietary</td>\n<td>Proprietary</td>\n<td>MIT</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>Maintainer responsiveness</td>\n<td>Direct via <a href=\"https://linkedin.com/in/askneelnow\" rel=\"nofollow\">@askneelnow</a></td>\n<td>Anthropic queue</td>\n<td>Composio queue</td>\n<td>Community</td>\n</tr>\n</tbody>\n</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>\n<hr>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Get started in 5 minutes (non-developer path)</h2><a id=\"user-content-get-started-in-5-minutes-non-developer-path\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Get started in 5 minutes (non-developer path)\" href=\"#get-started-in-5-minutes-non-developer-path\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Are you a marketer, agency owner, or content lead who doesn't live in a terminal?</strong> Here's the fastest path:</p>\n<ol dir=\"auto\">\n<li><strong>Open <a href=\"https://claude.com/cowork\" rel=\"nofollow\">Anthropic Cowork</a></strong> in your browser (no installation, no terminal, no command line). Sign up free if you don't have an account.</li>\n<li><strong>Click your profile menu → Settings → Plugins → Add Marketplace.</strong> Paste: <code>indranilbanerjee/neels-plugins</code></li>\n<li><strong>Find \"Digital Marketing Pro\" in the list → click Install.</strong></li>\n<li><strong>Type in chat:</strong> <em>\"Let's set up a brand for ACME Corp\"</em> — Claude will walk you through brand setup (voice, audience, jurisdiction, competitors).</li>\n<li><strong>Then ask:</strong> <em>\"Run a full marketing engagement for ACME\"</em> — and watch ~50–60 strategy documents get produced over the next ~60 minutes.</li>\n</ol>\n<p dir=\"auto\">That's it. You never touched a command line. Your team Drive will hold the outputs. Re-open Cowork tomorrow and pick up where you left off.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>If you're more technical</strong>, see <a href=\"#quick-start\">Quick start</a> below for the Claude Code CLI install (one terminal command).</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>For team usage (agencies running 50+ brands)</strong>, also run <code>/digital-marketing-pro:cowork-setup</code> once so brand state persists across Cowork sessions via your team's Google Drive.</p>\n<hr>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Why Digital Marketing Pro</h2><a id=\"user-content-why-digital-marketing-pro\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Why Digital Marketing Pro\" href=\"#why-digital-marketing-pro\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Most AI marketing tools generate isolated outputs — a campaign brief here, an email there. No canonical sequence, no shared state, no enforced structure. Result: inconsistent depth, missed dependencies, outputs that don't compound.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>DM Pro runs every brand through the same 12 parts, producing the same files in the same order, with explicit dependency rules between them.</strong> That's the whole product. Everything else — the 158 skills, 24 agents, May–June 2026 compliance updates, Cowork persistence — exists to make that 12-Part Flow ship cleanly for real agencies on real client work.</p>\n<markdown-accessiblity-table data-catalyst=\"\"><table>\n<thead>\n<tr>\n<th>What this gives you that ad-hoc prompts don't</th>\n<th>Why it matters</th>\n</tr>\n</thead>\n<tbody>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>Canonical 12-Part Strategy Flow</strong> producing the Four Core Documents (61 explicit steps)</td>\n<td>Every engagement looks the same, so handoffs work and quality is auditable</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>Two-Views Model</strong> (v1 unbiased + v2 client-validated)</td>\n<td>You never lose the original market view when the client pushes back</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>Decision Matrix</strong> — maps validation responses to re-runs</td>\n<td>Stops over-running (wasted hours) and under-running (broken strategy)</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>Living Project Instruction File</strong> — single source of truth per engagement</td>\n<td>All skills read it first; corrections propagate automatically</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>EU AI Act Article 50 readiness</strong> built in</td>\n<td>C2PA provenance signing, deepfake disclosure, final Article 50 Guidelines + Code of Practice (10 June 2026) in compliance</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td><strong>6-platform AEO/GEO audit</strong> (incl. Google AI Mode)</td>\n<td>The first marketing plugin to treat AI Mode as a distinct surface from AI Overviews</td>\n</tr>\n</tbody>\n</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>\n<hr>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">What you get in 60 minutes</h2><a id=\"user-content-what-you-get-in-60-minutes\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: What you get in 60 minutes\" href=\"#what-you-get-in-60-minutes\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Run <code>/digital-marketing-pro:engagement</code> and the plugin produces a full brand-strategy engagement in roughly 60 minutes on Opus 4.8/Opus 5-class models — <strong>~50–60 canonical files</strong> organized by part:</p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li><strong>Part 1</strong> — Stone-vs-Opinion intake (what the client knows for certain vs what they believe)</li>\n<li><strong>Part 2</strong> — External market research (unbiased, no client docs)</li>\n<li><strong>Part 3</strong> — Four Core Documents — 61 explicit steps across Business &amp; SBU Analysis, Segmentation Framework, Brand Positioning &amp; Communications, DMFlow</li>\n<li><strong>Part 4</strong> — Competitive + Customer + Market analysis (4 unbiased docs)</li>\n<li><strong>Part 5</strong> — Client Validation Document — the one true stop</li>\n<li><strong>Part 6</strong> — Selective v2 re-runs per Decision Matrix</li>\n<li><strong>Part 7</strong> — Preparation documents (campaign architecture, KPI tree, content pillars, approval chains)</li>\n<li><strong>Part 8</strong> — <strong>Growth Plan + 12-month Yearly Planner</strong> (the flagship deliverable)</li>\n<li><strong>Part 9</strong> — Channel-strategy fan-out (up to 17 channel docs in 7 families)</li>\n<li><strong>Part 10</strong> — Execution artefacts (ad copy, post copy, headlines, CTAs)</li>\n<li><strong>Part 11</strong> — AI creative briefs (with Nano Banana Pro / Veo 3.1 / Gemini Omni model guidance and C2PA + deepfake-disclosure clauses)</li>\n<li><strong>Part 12</strong> — Continuous improvement loop</li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Cost: roughly <strong>$15–40 in Claude API spend</strong> for a full 12-part engagement using Opus 4.8 or Opus 5 (same $5/$25 per-MTok pricing). The plugin itself is MIT-licensed and free.</p>\n<hr>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Quick start</h2><a id=\"user-content-quick-start\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Quick start\" href=\"#quick-start\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">1. All 158 SKILL.md files in DM Pro are platform-portable as written. The sibling manifests are thin platform-specific wrappers around the same <code>skills/</code> directory — no skill duplication, no maintenance fork. The pattern is borrowed from Google's reference repo <a href=\"https://github.com/gemini-cli-extensions/data-agent-kit-starter-pack\"><code>gemini-cli-extensions/data-agent-kit-starter-pack</code></a>.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Minimum Claude Code version: 2.1.157</strong> (declared via <code>requiredMinimumVersion</code> in plugin.json — landed in Claude Code v2.1.163, June 4 2026). Older Claude Code builds will be told to upgrade rather than load DMP with missing features.</p>\n<hr>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Works on 40+ agent harnesses (via the Agent Skills open standard)</h2><a id=\"user-content-works-on-40-agent-harnesses-via-the-agent-skills-open-standard\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Works on 40+ agent harnesses (via the Agent Skills open standard)\" href=\"#works-on-40-agent-harnesses-via-the-agent-skills-open-standard\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Beyond the 8 surfaces above where we ship a native manifest, DMP's 158 <code>SKILL.md</code> files work out-of-the-box on any agent that adopted the <a href=\"https://agentskills.io\" rel=\"nofollow\">Agent Skills open standard</a> (Anthropic-published Dec 2025, 41+ adopters as of June 2026). On each platform below, point it at our <code>skills/</code> folder and all 158 marketing skills are immediately discoverable. No platform-specific manifest needed.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Tier 1 — verified-compatible platforms with explicit Agent Skills install paths:</strong></p>\n<markdown-accessiblity-table Shipping 35 extra wrapper manifests would create maintenance overhead with zero added value.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">If you run into a platform-specific install snag, file a <a href=\"https://github.com/indranilbanerjee/digital-marketing-pro/issues\">GitHub issue</a> — we'll add platform-specific docs as users report patterns.</p>\n<hr>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">The 12-Part Engagement Methodology</h2><a id=\"user-content-the-12-part-engagement-methodology\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: The 12-Part Engagement Methodology\" href=\"#the-12-part-engagement-methodology\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<markdown-accessiblity-table data-catalyst=\"\"><table>\n<thead>\n<tr>\n<th>Part</th>\n<th>Name</th>\n<th>Output</th>\n</tr>\n</thead>\n<tbody>\n<tr>\n<td>1</td>\n<td>Client Inputs</td>\n<td>Stone vs Opinion intake (what client knows for certain vs what they believe)</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>2</td>\n<td>External Research</td>\n<td>Unbiased market research (no client docs used)</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>3</td>\n<td><strong>Four Core Documents</strong></td>\n<td>61 explicit steps — Business &amp; SBU (18), Segmentation (15), Brand Positioning (19), DMFlow (9)</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>4</td>\n<td>Competitive + Customer + Market</td>\n<td>4 unbiased analysis documents (4.1–4.4)</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>5</td>\n<td><strong>Client Validation Document</strong></td>\n<td>The one true stop — client accepts/rejects/edits each finding</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>6</td>\n<td>Selective v2 Re-runs</td>\n<td>Subset of Part 3 + Part 4 docs re-run per the Decision Matrix</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>7</td>\n<td>Preparation Documents</td>\n<td>Internal operating layer (campaign architecture, KPI tree, content pillars, asset inventory, approval chains)</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>8</td>\n<td><strong>Growth Plan + Yearly Planner</strong></td>\n<td>The flagship 11-section client-facing strategy + 12-month operational calendar</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>9</td>\n<td>Channel Strategy Fan-out</td>\n<td>Up to 17 channel docs grouped into 7 families</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>10</td>\n<td>Execution Artefacts</td>\n<td>Ad copy, post copy, headlines, CTAs</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>11</td>\n<td>AI Creative Instructions</td>\n<td>Visual asset briefs with C2PA + EU Article 50 clauses</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>12</td>\n<td><strong>Continuous Improvement Loop</strong></td>\n<td>Quarterly briefs feeding signals back into product/offering decisions</td>\n</tr>\n</tbody>\n</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Key architectural concepts:</strong></p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li><strong>Two-Views Model</strong> — Every engagement carries v1 (unbiased market view) and v2 (client-validated view) after Part 5. Operating decisions reference v2; ideation references both. v1 is never deleted.</li>\n<li><strong>Stone vs Opinion</strong> — Every fact captured at intake is tagged with confidence. Stone = client knows for certain. Opinion = client believes (becomes a research question, not ground truth).</li>\n<li><strong>Decision Matrix</strong> — Maps client validation responses to which v1 documents need v2 re-runs. Prevents over- and under-re-running.</li>\n<li><strong>Update-Back Rule</strong> — Live operations surface corrections → source documents get versioned (v2.1, v2.2 …) → Living Project Instruction File propagates the change to all downstream skills.</li>\n<li><strong>Living Project Instruction File</strong> — Single source of truth per engagement. All skills read it first.</li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\">15+ strategic-framework reference documents in <code>skills/context-engine/</code> support the methodology (Five Digital Markets, Channel Families, In-Market vs Out-Market, Multi-Dimensional Decision Framework, Unit Economics, Actionable Persona Format, B2B Decision-Making Unit, Three-Scenario Forecasting, 30/60/90-Day Framework, Reporting Cadence, Fixed vs Variable Budget, Competitor 3-Question Output, India Market Context, and more).</p>\n<hr>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">What's new</h2><a id=\"user-content-whats-new\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: What's new\" href=\"#whats-new\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">DM Pro is updated against the <strong>actual current marketing ecosystem state</strong> — the July 2026 market refresh (GPT-5.6 Sol/Terra/Luna, the Claude 5 family, the <strong>final</strong> EU AI Act Article 50 guidelines + Transparency Code of Practice), Google I/O 2026, the Google Ads v25 / Meta v25 API shifts, and the latest AI image/video model landscape. No \"trained on 2024 data\" surprises in your client outputs.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>v3.17.1 — Registry reconciliation + anonymity guard (July 30)</strong></p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Balanced/fast model aliases re-pointed to the current generation (claude-sonnet-5, gpt-5.6-terra, gpt-5.6-luna); GPT-5.5/5.4 family marked <code>supported</code> with <code>replacement_id</code>s targeting GPT-5.6; <code>balanced-video</code> tier added. New <code>tests/test_source_anonymity.py</code> machine-enforces the never-name-the-source-organization rule (needles assembled at runtime; verified to fire on a planted probe). Suite: 210 tests.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>v3.17.0 — The Line-by-Line Audit (July 29)</strong></p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Full-repo audit: 16 parallel readers covered 100% of the repo's files line-by-line, cross-checked against primary-source July-2026 facts and against the code itself. Highlights: every doc↔script contract verified (payload shapes, flags, thresholds, storage paths); dead products removed from recommendations; EU Code/Article 50 language moved fully to final-Code, post-deadline state; scripts hardened (trustworthy exit codes, atomic writes, real quality gates, input sanitization); reference-file indexes completed so all 169 reference docs are discoverable; self-containment guard now covers the entire repo. Suite: 209 tests.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>v3.16.0 — July Market Refresh (July 12)</strong>\nEverything verified against primary sources on ship day. The <strong>final EU Code of Practice on Transparency of AI-Generated Content</strong> (10 June 2026) replaces all second-draft guidance in the compliance docs — standardized EU disclosure icons are live, the initial-signatory window closed <strong>22 July 2026</strong> (late signing remains possible), and the final Article 50 Guidelines are in (<code>compliance-rules.md</code>). The model registry adds the <strong>Claude 5 family</strong> (<code>claude-fable-5</code> with refusal/fallback semantics, <code>claude-opus-5</code> — the new <code>latest-text-anthropic</code>, <code>claude-sonnet-5</code>) and <strong>GPT-5.6 Sol/Terra/Luna</strong> (<code>latest-text-openai</code> → Sol), flags Opus 4.1's Aug 5 retirement, and is mirrored to the shared suite registry. Paid docs get the <strong>Meta v25 truth pass</strong> (standalone Advantage+ Shopping/App creation blocked via API — unified Advantage+ documented as the go-forward path; Page Viewer metric replaces legacy reach) plus Google Ads v25 breaking changes and LinkedIn 202607. <code>gsc-ai-performance</code> adds the Discover generative surfaces. README claims rotated to the current model lineup.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>v3.15.1 — Self-containment patch (July 12)</strong>\nRemoved every cross-plugin capability reference from the skill surface: <code>ad-creative</code> and the engagement's Part 11 hand visual production to your own tooling (design team, AI generators, or connected design platforms) instead of naming a sibling plugin; <code>launch-campaign</code> C2PA signing and checkpoint-resume route through DMP's own <code>c2pa-metadata</code> and <code>/digital-marketing-pro:resume</code>; <code>validate-profile</code> checks DMP's own publish dir (<code>$DIGITAL_MARKETING_PRO_PUBLISH_DIR</code> / <code>~/Documents/DigitalMarketingPro/</code>); the EU compliance reference docs are DMP-centric. DMP is fully standalone — no other plugin is ever required for any documented capability. Cross-promo links in this README stay; capability delegation is gone.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>v3.15.0 — Reliability &amp; Truth (July 7)</strong>\nA full-repo audit (orchestration, agents, skills, scripts, docs/manifests) surfaced ~200 findings, all fixed in one pass. One shared workspace-root/slugify/atomic-write helper (<code>_common.py</code>) ends the storage split-brain; connectors are honestly opt-in (the shipped <code>.mcp.json</code> is empty, fictional npm packages purged, memory backends demoted to \"only if connected\"); all 18 execution skills carry a uniform <code>## Execution gate</code> and flip to <code>disable-model-invocation: false</code> (<strong>closes issue #6</strong>); the Tessl workflow moves to the <code>tessl review</code> CLI + <code>.github/tessl-rubric.yml</code> (<strong>closes issue #8</strong>); <code>competitor-intelligence</code> merges into <code>competitive-intel</code> (<strong>25 → 24 agents</strong>); <code>embed-c2pa.py</code> gains the EU AI Act Article 50 <code>--ai-disclosure</code> assertion; and a new <code>check_skill_contracts.py</code> doc-vs-argparse linter + state-layer tests grow the suite <strong>123 → 207 passing</strong>. Every fabricated capability, stale count, and phantom flag is fixed or labeled.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>v3.14.1 — README sync + test-infra extension (June 28)</strong>\nPatch release fixing 4 stale references in this README that escaped the v3.14.0 ship: the Cowork badge anchor, the <code>## Supported surfaces</code> heading, a second internal anchor, and the missing v3.13.1 + v3.14.0 entries in this very section. The plugin is designed so that each skill produces numbered intermediate files (<code>01-...md</code>, <code>02-...md</code>, …, <code>PLAN.md</code>) under <code>${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA}/{brand}/seo/{workflow}/{date}/</code> — downstream skills read those numbered files, not the endpoint, so you can re-run any single step without redoing the whole chain.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Agency onboarding workflow</strong> (week 1 of a new client engagement):</p>\n<div class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\"><pre class=\"notranslate\"><code>1. /digital-marketing-pro:brand-setup\n2. /digital-marketing-pro:competitor-analysis        ← picks the right competitors for everything downstream\n3. See <a href=\"/indranilbanerjee/digital-marketing-pro/blob/main/CONNECTORS.md\">CONNECTORS.md</a> and <a href=\"/indranilbanerjee/digital-marketing-pro/blob/main/docs/integrations-guide.md\">Integrations Guide</a>.</p>\n<hr>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Resumable workflows + visible output folder (v3.7.7+)</h2><a id=\"user-content-resumable-workflows--visible-output-folder-v377\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Resumable workflows + visible output folder (v3.7.7+)\" href=\"#resumable-workflows--visible-output-folder-v377\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Two user-team complaints from the v3.7.5 cycle drove this release: \"dm pro is taking too long to process\" (the 60-minute engagement that breaks midway loses 30+ minutes of work on restart) and the general \"where did my 50 deliverable files save?\" confusion (everything was landing under the Windows-hidden <code>~/.claude-marketing/</code> dotfolder).</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Fix 1 — Resumable workflows.</strong> Every long-running DMP workflow now writes per-part checkpoints to disk so an interrupted session can resume from the next un-checkpointed part instead of restarting from Part 1. Covered workflows: <code>engagement</code> (12-Part Strategy Flow), <code>campaign-plan</code>, <code>content-engine</code>, <code>seo-audit</code>, <code>competitor-analysis</code>, <code>campaign-audit</code> (v3.7.5), <code>launch-campaign</code> (v3.7.5), plus a <code>custom</code> slot for any other long flow. skills depend on 14 actions that map to real marketing APIs (Google Ads, Meta Marketing, LinkedIn, TikTok, HubSpot, Salesforce, Klaviyo, Mailchimp, Customer.io, Gmail, Cision, Muckrack, Slack, Google Calendar, Ahrefs, Similarweb, SEMrush, Google Search Console). v3.7.5–v3.7.7 shipped these actions as honest stubs that always returned <code>status: stub_implementation</code> regardless of which connectors the user had. v3.7.10 introduces a resolver that probes the live state and resolves each action to one of three modes per call:</p>\n<markdown-accessiblity-table data-catalyst=\"\"><table>\n<thead>\n<tr>\n<th>mode</th>\n<th>what it means</th>\n</tr>\n</thead>\n<tbody>\n<tr>\n<td><code>real</code></td>\n<td>runs end-to-end with no external API (currently only <code>arm-watchdog</code> which writes a watchdog config to <code>~/.claude-marketing/{brand}/watchdogs/</code>)</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td><code>manifest_ready</code></td>\n<td>a matching connector is configured — the response includes the exact HTTP request manifest (method, URL, headers, body template, auth pattern) for the orchestrator (Claude via MCP) to execute. Write/launch ops set <code>approval_required: true</code>.</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td><code>stub_unconfigured</code></td>\n<td>no matching connector is configured — the response includes the manual fallback PLUS copy-paste <code>.mcp.json</code> snippet, env-var list, and a Cowork-compatibility note</td>\n</tr>\n</tbody>\n</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Check what's live in your environment any time:</p>\n<div class=\"snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\"><pre class=\"notranslate\"><code>/digital-marketing-pro:doctor                              # A fuller 27-scenario development harness (14 actions × unconfigured / configured / local-execution variants) was used while building this out, but it is a dev tool and is not shipped in the repo.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Implementation:</strong> <code>scripts/_connector_registry.py</code> (catalog of 33 connectors, 11 categories, <code>is_connector_configured()</code> probe) + <code>scripts/connector_resolver.py</code> (<code>ACTION_SPECS</code> map + per-action manifest builders + local executors) + <code>scripts/action-doctor.py</code> (the doctor command's underlying script).</p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">v3.7.11 — actions can actually fire HTTP requests from Python</h3><a id=\"user-content-v3711--actions-can-actually-fire-http-requests-from-python\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: v3.7.11 — actions can actually fire HTTP requests from Python\" href=\"#v3711--actions-can-actually-fire-http-requests-from-python\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 dir=\"auto\">The v3.7.10 resolver returned a manifest of \"what would be sent.\" v3.7.11 adds <code>scripts/connector_executor.py</code> (stdlib <code>urllib.request</code>, no third-party deps) that takes that manifest and <strong>actually executes</strong> the request against the real API. Public CLI: <code>/digital-marketing-pro:execute-action</code>.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Executes end-to-end from Python (8 connectors, verified vendor docs):</strong></p>\n<markdown-accessiblity-table data-catalyst=\"\"><table>\n<thead>\n<tr>\n<th>Connector</th>\n<th>Env var</th>\n<th>What it can fire</th>\n</tr>\n</thead>\n<tbody>\n<tr>\n<td>Slack</td>\n<td><code>SLACK_BOT_TOKEN</code></td>\n<td><code>POST chat.postMessage</code> (with <code>body.ok</code> post-check)</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>HubSpot</td>\n<td><code>HUBSPOT_PRIVATE_APP_TOKEN</code></td>\n<td><code>GET /automation/v4/flows</code>, <code>POST /marketing/v3/campaigns</code></td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>Klaviyo</td>\n<td><code>KLAVIYO_PRIVATE_KEY</code></td>\n<td><code>GET /api/flows</code>, <code>PATCH /api/flows/{id}</code> (vnd.api+json)</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>SendGrid</td>\n<td><code>SENDGRID_API_KEY</code></td>\n<td><code>POST /v3/mail/send</code> (202 success)</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>Brevo</td>\n<td><code>BREVO_API_KEY</code></td>\n<td><code>POST /v3/smtp/email</code> (lowercase <code>api-key:</code> header)</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>Customer.io</td>\n<td><code>CUSTOMERIO_APP_API_KEY</code></td>\n<td><code>POST /v1/send/email</code> (App API key only)</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>Mailchimp</td>\n<td><code>MAILCHIMP_API_KEY</code></td>\n<td><code>GET /3.0/automations</code> (Basic auth, dc from suffix)</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>Ahrefs</td>\n<td><code>AHREFS_API_KEY</code></td>\n<td><code>GET /v3/site-explorer/metrics</code></td>\n</tr>\n</tbody>\n</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Requires the MCP path (25 OAuth-only connectors):</strong> Google Ads, Meta Marketing, LinkedIn Marketing, LinkedIn Publishing, TikTok Ads, Twitter/X, Gmail, Google Calendar, Google Analytics, Google Search Console, Meta Graph, Salesforce, Pipedrive, Zoho CRM, Buffer, Hootsuite, Cision, Muckrack, Amplitude, Similarweb, SEMrush, Moz, Intercom, Canva, Figma. For all of these, the resolver still returns <code>manifest_ready</code> so you can see the exact HTTP shape Claude's MCP tool will send — Python just can't execute the OAuth flow itself.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Safety gates:</strong> read ops auto-execute with <code>--execute</code>; write ops require both <code>--execute --confirm</code>; missing env vars block with <code>setup_hint_credential</code>; unresolved <code>{VAR}</code> placeholders block before the request fires. Every fired call logs to <code>~/.claude-marketing/{brand}/executions/</code>.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Test coverage:</strong> end-to-end HTTP send-and-receive for the 8 connectors (Slack <code>body.ok</code> post-check, Klaviyo vnd.api+json, Brevo lowercase header, Mailchimp Basic, plus the safety gates and data substitution) was validated during development against a stdlib <code>http.server</code> mock. That mock harness is a dev tool and is not shipped in the repo; the shipped suite covers the resolver layer via <code>tests/test_connector_resolver.py</code>.</p>\n<hr>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Model curator — no hardcoded model ids (v3.7.4+)</h2><a id=\"user-content-model-curator--no-hardcoded-model-ids-v374\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Model curator — no hardcoded model ids (v3.7.4+)\" href=\"#model-curator--no-hardcoded-model-ids-v374\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Frontier models change every ~6 weeks. Hardcoding <code>claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929</code> or <code>gemini-2.0-flash</code> across dozens of scripts means a provider deprecation silently 404s, the user blames the plugin, and the maintainer has to grep three repos. So we don't hardcode.</p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li><strong><code>scripts/model_registry.json</code></strong> — single source of truth for every model id used by the plugin, with vendor, tier, modality, status, and <code>replacement_id</code> for deprecated entries.</li>\n<li><strong><code>scripts/resolve_model.py</code></strong> — Python module + CLI. Resolves human aliases (<code>latest-balanced-anthropic</code>, <code>latest-fast-anthropic</code>, <code>latest-text-openai</code>, <code>latest-vision-google</code>, <code>latest-image-google</code>, <code>latest-video-google</code>) to concrete ids at call time. Deprecated ids passed via <code>--model</code> auto-fall-forward to their replacement (with a stderr warning).</li>\n<li><strong><code>scripts/refresh_models.py</code></strong> — polls Anthropic / OpenAI / Google / Evolink list endpoints with your API keys and reports drift versus the registry (NEW models in the provider catalog, STALE models in the registry).</li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Every script that calls a provider model now accepts <code>--model</code> (or <code>--openai-model</code> / <code>--anthropic-model</code> for <code>scripts/ai-visibility-checker.py</code>) and the value is validated against the registry. See <a href=\"/indranilbanerjee/digital-marketing-pro/blob/main/docs/MODEL-CURATOR.md\"><code>docs/MODEL-CURATOR.md</code></a> for the full alias map, curation policy, and worked examples.</p>\n<div Coverage:</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">🇪🇺 EU (GDPR + AI Act Article 50) · 🇺🇸 US Federal (CAN-SPAM) · 🇺🇸 California (CCPA/CPRA) · US 20+ state privacy laws · 🇨🇦 Canada (CASL + PIPEDA) · 🇧🇷 Brazil (LGPD) · 🇬🇧 UK (UK GDPR + PECR) · 🇦🇺 Australia (Privacy Act + Spam Act) · 🇸🇬 Singapore (PDPA) · 🇨🇳 China (PIPL) · 🇮🇳 India (DPDPA) · 🇯🇵 Japan (APPI) · 🇰🇷 South Korea (PIPA) · 🇸🇦 Saudi Arabia (PDPL) · 🇦🇪 UAE (Federal Decree-Law No. 45) · 🇹🇭 Thailand (PDPA)</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>EU AI Act Article 50 readiness (applicable 2 August 2026):</strong></p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>C2PA content-provenance signing via <code>/digital-marketing-pro:c2pa-metadata</code> (end-to-end tested against c2pa-python 0.32 — 75-byte test PNG → 42,818-byte signed PNG with <code>manifest_embedded_and_verified=true</code>)</li>\n<li>Pre-publish gate (<code>/digital-marketing-pro:check</code>) treats missing C2PA on AI-flagged assets in EU campaigns as CRITICAL → BLOCKED</li>\n<li>Final Article 50 Guidelines + final Code of Practice on Transparency of AI-Generated Content (10 June 2026) in <code>compliance-rules.md</code> §1.1b.i with six-row clarification table + five-point action list</li>\n<li>Production cert guide at <code>docs/c2pa-production-cert-guide.md</code> covers the four CAI-recognised authorities (Adobe Content Credentials, Truepic, Numbers Protocol, Microsoft Azure Confidential Ledger)</li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Other May 2026 regulatory updates baked in:</strong></p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>NY synthetic-performer disclosure law (live June 2026, $1K–$5K per violation, $10K repeat) — applies to synthetic influencers + AI endorsements</li>\n<li>FTC May 2026 endorsement guidance — synthetic influencers, AI testimonials, AI-edited creator content</li>\n<li>CJEU March 2026 ruling — pseudonymized cookie IDs are personal data when re-identification is feasible</li>\n<li>CCPA Jan 2026 ADMT amendments + AI-derived sensitive data classification</li>\n<li>DPDP Phase II preparation — consent manager registration opens Nov 2026</li>\n</ul>\n<hr>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">AEO / GEO — May 2026 reality</h2><a id=\"user-content-aeo--geo--may-2026-reality\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: AEO / GEO — May 2026 dir=\"auto\">The search landscape pivoted hard in 2025–2026:</p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li>Google AI Overviews appear on ~55% of all Google searches (Seer Interactive, Sept 2025); organic CTR on AI Overview queries dropped ~61% (1.76% → 0.61%); ~58% of Google searches are now zero-click</li>\n<li><strong>Google AI Mode</strong> became the default conversational search experience for opted-in users at I/O 2026 (19 May 2026), backed by Gemini 3.5 Flash — ~1B MAUs</li>\n<li>ChatGPT search reaches ~883M MAU; Perplexity heavily skews citations to Reddit (47% of factual cites); Wikipedia drives 48% of ChatGPT citations</li>\n</ul>\n<p dir=\"auto\">DM Pro's AEO/GEO skills (<code>/digital-marketing-pro:aeo-audit</code>, <code>:geo-monitor</code>, <code>:entity-audit</code>) reflect this:</p>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li><strong>6-platform audit standard</strong> — ChatGPT, Perplexity, Google AI Mode, Google AI Overviews, Gemini, Microsoft Copilot (was 5 — AI Mode added May 2026)</li>\n<li>Schema strategy refresh — Google's March 2026 core update demoted FAQ/Review/HowTo schema on non-primary pages. Skills emphasise entity-rich JSON-LD (Article + Organization + Person + Product) and offer an optional <strong>LLMs.txt</strong> companion (Google states it is not required for AI-features eligibility)</li>\n<li>Citation tracking across all 6 surfaces, with Profound / Otterly / Conductor AgentStack integration paths in the connectors layer</li>\n<li><strong>Share of AI Voice</strong> as a first-class metric in <code>/digital-marketing-pro:performance-report</code></li>\n</ul>\n<hr>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Channel guidance — May 2026 updates baked in</h2><a id=\"user-content-channel-guidance--may-2026-updates-baked-in\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Channel guidance — May 2026 updates baked in\" href=\"#channel-guidance--may-2026-updates-baked-in\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<ul dir=\"auto\">\n<li><strong>LinkedIn (March 2026 algorithm shift):</strong> external links and engagement bait penalized ~60%. New <strong>Depth Score</strong> measures dwell time. Followers no longer guarantee reach. Skills optimize for relevance and Depth Score.</li>\n<li><strong>Email:</strong> Apple MPP affects ~64% of B2C opens — open rate is functionally dead as a primary KPI. <strong>DMARC + RFC 8058 one-click POST unsubscribe</strong> mandatory; non-compliant bulk mail to Gmail/Yahoo/Microsoft gets permanent 550 rejections. Spam threshold tightened to &lt;0.10%.</li>\n<li><strong>TikTok (post Jan 22 2026 USDS Joint Venture closing):</strong> US data + algorithm under USDS LLC; ByteDance retains &lt;20%. AI-generated creators require disclosure label; AI content excluded from Creator Rewards Program; daily shoppable-post limits effective May 11 2026.</li>\n<li><strong>Meta Advantage+ Leads</strong> (global as of May 2026), <strong>Threads ads</strong> (global, image-only), <strong>brand-safety inventory tiers</strong> (Expanded/Moderate/Limited — Limited costs ~30% reach).</li>\n<li><strong>WhatsApp</strong> per-message pricing (since 1 July 2025) — India marketing template ≈ USD 0.0118 per message; 72-hour free service window from CTWA ads or Page CTAs.</li>\n<li><strong>Third-party cookies — deprecation cancelled.</strong> First-party data is the strategic priority. The <code>attribution-model</code> skill defaults to first-party + MMM + incrementality stack.</li>\n<li><strong>Sora dependency note:</strong> OpenAI consumer Sora app discontinued April 26 2026; Sora API September 24 2026. AI creative briefs default to <strong>Veo 3.1, Kling v3.0 Pro, Runway Gen-4, Gemini Omni</strong>.</li>\n</ul>\n<hr>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Q: How does this compare to LangChain marketing templates / CrewAI marketing crews / general AI marketing tools?</strong>\nThose are frameworks. DM Pro is a <strong>packaged, opinionated methodology</strong> with explicit dependency rules between every output. You can build something like it in LangChain or CrewAI — at the cost of months of engineering. DM Pro ships it.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Q: Which Claude interface should I use?</strong></p>\n<markdown-accessiblity-table data-catalyst=\"\"><table>\n<thead>\n<tr>\n<th></th>\n<th align=\"center\">Claude Code</th>\n<th align=\"center\">Claude Cowork</th>\n<th align=\"center\">Claude Desktop (no Cowork)</th>\n<th align=\"center\">claude.ai web</th>\n</tr>\n</thead>\n<tbody>\n<tr>\n<td>Full plugin support</td>\n<td align=\"center\">yes</td>\n<td align=\"center\">yes</td>\n<td align=\"center\">partial</td>\n<td align=\"center\">no</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>Brand memory</td>\n<td align=\"center\">yes</td>\n<td align=\"center\">yes</td>\n<td align=\"center\">no</td>\n<td align=\"center\">no</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>MCP integrations</td>\n<td align=\"center\">all</td>\n<td align=\"center\">HTTP only</td>\n<td align=\"center\">HTTP only</td>\n<td align=\"center\">no</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>Document creation (Excel, PPT)</td>\n<td align=\"center\">no</td>\n<td align=\"center\">yes</td>\n<td align=\"center\">no</td>\n<td align=\"center\">no</td>\n</tr>\n<tr>\n<td>Recommended for</td>\n<td align=\"center\">Terminal workflows + scripting</td>\n<td align=\"center\">Visual desktop workflows</td>\n<td align=\"center\">Quick content</td>\n<td align=\"center\">One-off questions</td>\n</tr>\n</tbody>\n</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Q: How much does a full engagement cost in API spend?</strong>\nRoughly <strong>$15–40</strong> for a complete 12-part engagement using Opus 4.8 or Opus 5 (same pricing) across ~50–60 documents. Track per-brand consumption via Claude Code v2.1.149+ <code>/usage</code> (now integrated into <code>/digital-marketing-pro:agency-dashboard</code>).</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Q: Can I run multiple brands in parallel?</strong>\nYes. Each brand has its own <code>~/.claude-marketing/&lt;brand-slug&gt;/</code> directory and Python script state. Switch with <code>/digital-marketing-pro:switch-brand</code>.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Q: What if I only want a campaign plan, not the full methodology?</strong>\nSkip to <code>/digital-marketing-pro:campaign-plan</code>. Every individual surface (campaign / SEO / content / competitor / email / report) is independently runnable. The full engagement is the canonical path, not the only path.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Q: Will this work on Codex / Cursor / Copilot CLI / Antigravity?</strong>\nYes — verified-real native manifests ship for all 8 surfaces (CC, Cowork, Codex, Cursor, Copilot CLI, Antigravity, Hermes Agent, OpenClaw). See <a href=\"#supported-surfaces-v3171\">Supported surfaces</a> above for per-platform install commands.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Q: I run my team on Anthropic Cowork. Does brand state persist between sessions?</strong>\nYes — but you need to run <code>/digital-marketing-pro:cowork-setup</code> once per team first (v3.12.0). Cowork's per-session filesystem is ephemeral, and <code>${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA}</code> is too (<a href=\"https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/51398\" data-hovercard-type=\"issue\" data-hovercard-url=\"/anthropics/claude-code/issues/51398/hovercard\" aria-keyshortcuts=\"Alt+ArrowUp\">open issue #51398</a>). The setup wizard routes brand profiles + plans + reports through a Google Drive MCP so everything survives across sessions and is shared across the team. Multi-team isolation via per-team folder names.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Q: What happens when a new Claude / OpenAI / Google model ships? Do I need to update the plugin?</strong>\nNo — the plugin uses a shared model curator (<code>scripts/resolve_model.py</code>) that resolves aliases (<code>latest-balanced-anthropic</code>, <code>latest-text-google</code>, etc.) at call time. When a model is deprecated, the curator auto-falls-forward to the replacement. <code>/digital-marketing-pro:doctor</code> reports registry age and severity (<code>ok</code> &lt;60d / <code>warn</code> 60-119d / <code>urgent</code> ≥120d); when stale it prints the exact <code>python scripts/refresh_models.py</code> invocation to poll the provider APIs for drift. Plus <code>settings.json.example</code> ships a <code>fallbackModel</code> chain so Claude Code transparently swaps models on overload.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Q: Is this an Anthropic product?</strong>\nNo — independent open-source plugin built by <a href=\"https://indranil.in\" rel=\"nofollow\">Indranil Banerjee</a>. MIT-licensed. Runs on Claude Code + Cowork.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Q: I found a compliance rule that looks out of date.</strong>\n<a href=\"https://github.com/indranilbanerjee/digital-marketing-pro/issues\">File an issue</a> with the citation. Privacy and AI law change quarterly — DM Pro is actively maintained against the May 2026 reality but enforcement actions and amendments keep coming.</p>\n<hr>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Troubleshooting</h2><a id=\"user-content-troubleshooting\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Troubleshooting\" supported platforms, with the fix.</p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Claude Code + Cowork</h3><a id=\"user-content-claude-code--cowork\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Claude Code + Cowork\" href=\"#claude-code--cowork\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>\"/plugin isn't available in this environment\"</strong>\nYou're in the standard Claude chat app (browser <code>claude.ai</code> or the Claude Desktop app). The <code>/plugin</code> slash command only works in <strong>Claude Code</strong> (the dev CLI/IDE at <a href=\"https://claude.com/code\" rel=\"nofollow\">claude.com/code</a>) and <strong>Anthropic Cowork</strong>. Everywhere else, plugins install via the UI: click the <strong>Plugins</strong> button at the bottom of the chat. See <a href=\"#updating\">Updating</a> for the full recovery procedure.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>\"Plugin installed but slash commands not showing\"</strong>\nRun <code>/reload-plugins</code> (Claude Code) or restart the Cowork chat session. If still missing, the installed version may be older than v2.1.157 — your Claude Code build needs to be on <strong>v2.1.157 or newer</strong> (DMP declares <code>requiredMinimumVersion: 2.1.157</code> in plugin.json). Run <code>claude --version</code> and update via <code>npm install -g @anthropic-ai/claude-code</code>.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>\"Brand profile vanishes between Cowork sessions\"</strong>\nCowork's filesystem is per-session ephemeral — <code>~/.claude-marketing/</code> AND <code>${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA}</code> both reset at session end (<a href=\"https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/51398\" data-hovercard-type=\"issue\" data-hovercard-url=\"/anthropics/claude-code/issues/51398/hovercard\" aria-keyshortcuts=\"Alt+ArrowUp\">open issue #51398</a>). Fix: run <code>/digital-marketing-pro:cowork-setup</code> once per team — it routes brand state through a Google Drive MCP so profiles survive across sessions and your whole team sees them. See <a href=\"#whats-new\">v3.12.0 release notes</a> for the why-and-how.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>\"<code>/digital-marketing-pro:doctor</code> says urgent (model registry stale)\"</strong>\nThe model registry hasn't been refreshed for 60+ days — frontier models shift every ~6 weeks so deprecated IDs may start 404-ing. Fix: <code>ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=... OPENAI_API_KEY=... GEMINI_API_KEY=... EVOLINK_API_KEY=... python scripts/refresh_models.py</code>. The script polls each provider's <code>/v1/models</code> endpoint and reports drift versus our registry.</p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">OpenAI Codex / Cursor / GitHub Copilot CLI / Antigravity</h3><a id=\"user-content-openai-codex--cursor--github-copilot-cli--antigravity\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: OpenAI Codex / Cursor / GitHub Copilot CLI / Antigravity\" href=\"#openai-codex--cursor--github-copilot-cli--antigravity\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>\"Skills install but commands not discovered\"</strong>\nThese platforms read SKILL.md by description match — invoke via natural language (<em>\"Run a competitor analysis on stripe.com\"</em>) rather than typing a slash command. Slash commands (<code>/digital-marketing-pro:&lt;name&gt;</code>) are a Claude Code convention; on other surfaces the agent picks up the skill from intent.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>\"Codex says skill name failed regex check\"</strong>\nCodex enforces <code>[a-z0-9-]+</code> on skill names. All 158 DMP skill names pass this regex (verified in the test suite). If you see this error, it's likely a personal skill you added — rename it to lowercase + hyphens only.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>\"Cursor <code>/add-plugin</code> returns 'plugin not found'\"</strong>\nUse the full Git URL form: <code>/add-plugin digital-marketing-pro@https://github.com/indranilbanerjee/digital-marketing-pro</code>. Cursor's marketplace integration is fastest, but the Git URL form always works.</p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Hermes Agent</h3><a id=\"user-content-hermes-agent\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Hermes Agent\" href=\"#hermes-agent\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>\"<code>hermes plugins install ...</code> finishes but no skills appear\"</strong>\nThe plugin needs to be enabled after install: <code>hermes plugins enable digital-marketing-pro</code>. Then verify with <code>hermes plugins list</code>. If the adapter's <code>register()</code> returned silently with no skills, run the audit: <code>cd ~/.hermes/plugins/digital-marketing-pro &amp;&amp; python __init__.py</code> — it'll print the discovered skill count + first 5 skills, confirming the clone got the full <code>skills/</code> tree.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>\"register_skill error in Hermes logs\"</strong>\nCheck Hermes version: this plugin targets <strong>v0.15.2+</strong>. Run <code>hermes --version</code>. Older builds may have a different <code>ctx</code> API surface — the adapter degrades gracefully (logs an error, doesn't crash) but won't register skills. Upgrade Hermes to the latest public preview.</p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">OpenClaw</h3><a id=\"user-content-openclaw\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: OpenClaw\" href=\"#openclaw\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>\"OpenClaw can't find the plugin manifest\"</strong>\nUse the <code>git:</code> install scheme: <code>openclaw plugins install git:github.com/indranilbanerjee/digital-marketing-pro</code>. If you used another scheme and it failed, the fallback is: <code>cd ~/.openclaw/plugins &amp;&amp; git clone https://github.com/indranilbanerjee/digital-marketing-pro &amp;&amp; openclaw plugins enable digital-marketing-pro</code>.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>\"OpenClaw uses Claude bundle but loses some features\"</strong>\nOpenClaw auto-detects our <code>.claude-plugin/plugin.json</code> as a Claude-compatible bundle, but the native <code>openclaw.plugin.json</code> gives first-class discoverability. Both load the same 158 skills from <code>./skills</code>. Verify with <code>openclaw plugins inspect digital-marketing-pro --runtime --json</code>.</p>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">General (any platform)</h3><a id=\"user-content-general-any-platform\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: General (any platform)\" href=\"#general-any-platform\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>\"Tests in <code>tests/</code> fail when I <code>git clone</code> locally\"</strong>\nRun <code>python tests/run_all.py</code> from the repo root. All 209 tests are stdlib-only — no <code>pip install</code> needed. If they fail, the most likely cause is a Python version mismatch (DMP supports Python 3.8+) or a clone that omitted some <code>skills/</code> subdirectories. Try <code>git clone --depth=1</code> again.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>\"<code>/digital-marketing-pro:doctor</code> shows my action as stub_unconfigured\"</strong>\nThat action needs an MCP connector configured. Run <code>python scripts/connector-status.py --action setup-guide --name &lt;connector-name&gt;</code> for the exact setup snippet. Add it to your <code>.mcp.json</code> under <code>mcpServers</code>, restart your agent, and the action becomes <code>manifest_ready</code>. See <a href=\"#connector-aware-action-resolver-v3710\">Connector-aware action resolver</a> for the full readiness model.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>\"Where do my brand files actually go?\"</strong>\nRun <code>/digital-marketing-pro:output-folder</code> — it prints the active output directory and (on local Claude Code) opens it in your OS file manager. Default: <code>~/.claude-marketing/&lt;brand-slug&gt;/</code> for working state + <code>~/Documents/DigitalMarketingPro/&lt;brand&gt;/</code> for finished deliverables. Both configurable via <code>output-folder</code>.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>Still stuck?</strong> <a href=\"https://github.com/indranilbanerjee/digital-marketing-pro/issues\">Open an issue</a> with the exact error message + platform name + version. We respond within a few days.</p>\n<hr>\n<div class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"><h2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\">Updating</h2><a id=\"user-content-updating\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Updating\" href=\"#updating\"><svg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"><path d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"></path></svg></a></div>\n<blockquote>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>If you see \"/plugin isn't available in this environment\"</strong> — you're in the standard <strong>Claude chat app</strong> (browser OR installed desktop app). The <code>/plugin</code> slash command is <strong>only</strong> supported in two environments: <strong>Claude Code</strong> (the developer CLI / IDE at <a href=\"https://claude.com/code\" rel=\"nofollow\">claude.com/code</a>, <code>npm install -g @anthropic-ai/claude-code</code>) and <strong>Anthropic Cowork</strong>. Everywhere else — <code>claude.ai</code> web chat, the Claude Desktop app, mobile — plugins are managed through the UI, not slash commands.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">The plugin IS installed (your DM Pro skills work); only the management command is unavailable. Fix:</p>\n<ol dir=\"auto\">\n<li><strong>In the chat UI</strong> — click the <strong>Plugins</strong> button at the bottom of the chat → <strong>Manage plugins</strong> → find Digital Marketing Pro → look for Update / Refresh / Remove. If no Update button, <strong>Remove</strong> then <strong>Add plugin</strong> → re-install from <code>indranilbanerjee/neels-plugins</code>. The re-pull fetches the latest version.</li>\n<li><strong>For slash-command management</strong> — switch to Claude Code (CLI or IDE) or Cowork. The plugin runs identically across every Anthropic surface; you're choosing where to type management commands.</li>\n</ol>\n<p dir=\"auto\">Once you're in Claude Code or Cowork, the rest of this The 12-Part Strategy Flow encodes the canonical sequence a real engagement actually needs — Stone-vs-Opinion intake, Four Core Documents, Client Validation, Two-Views Model, Decision Matrix, Growth Plan + Yearly Planner, channel fan-out, execution artefacts, creative briefs, continuous improvement loop. Once it's a plugin, every engagement looks the same, handoffs work, and quality is auditable. That's the whole product.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\">If DM Pro saves your team time, <a href=\"https://github.com/indranilbanerjee/digital-marketing-pro/stargazers\">⭐ star the repo</a> — it's the single most useful thing you can do to help other marketing teams discover it. Ships verified-real <code>.codex-plugin/plugin.json</code> (per the published OpenAI schema), <code>gemini-extension.json</code> (at repo root, per Google's <code>gemini-cli-extensions/data-agent-kit-starter-pack</code> reference pattern), <code>.cursor-plugin/plugin.json</code> (per the verified Cursor 2.5+ JSON Schema), and <code>.github/plugin/plugin.json</code> (verified GitHub Copilot CLI schema; Copilot also recognizes <code>.claude-plugin/plugin.json</code> as documented fallback). Adds <code>AGENTS.md</code> at root (auto-loaded by Codex + Antigravity + Copilot + Cursor agent context chains). All 157 skills share via the Agent Skills open standard — no skill duplication. Replaces the v3.6/v3.7 era invented manifests correctly removed in v3.7.13. Pre-flight verified: 190/190 skills pass the Codex <code>[a-z0-9-]</code> regex AND the SKILL.md frontmatter <code>name</code> field matches each folder.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>v3.7.13 (2026-05-26)</strong> — Honest positioning. Removed v3.6 / v3.7 era invented manifests (<code>.codex-plugin/</code>, <code>.cursor-plugin/</code>, <code>.antigravity/</code>) + <code>docs/cross-platform-install.md</code>. Research confirmed they did not match the platforms' actual install specs. Zero functional changes; the plugin behaved identically in Claude Code + Cowork.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>v3.7.1 (2026-05-24)</strong> — Polish + discoverability pass. Stale asset counts swept across multiple docs. plugin.json description corrected to 69 scripts (was 71). No functional changes; no breaking changes.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>v3.5.0 (2026-05-24)</strong> — May-2026-ecosystem modernisation pass. Six discrete updates: (1) Google AI Mode as 6th AEO/GEO surface; (2) May 2026 broad core algorithm update triage; (3) EU AI Act Article 50 draft implementing guidelines (8 May; 3 June consultation; 2 Aug enforcement); (4) Meta Advantage+ Leads global + Threads ads + brand-safety filters; (5) Gemini Omni + Nano Banana Pro + Veo 3.1 in creative briefs; (6) Claude Code v2.1.149+ <code>/usage</code> per-brand cost tracking in agency dashboard.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>v3.4.1 (2026-05-17)</strong> — Audit &amp; corrections pass on v3.4.0. Parallel-dispatch speedup claim softened from flat 6× to honest 4–6× parallelism / ~50–80% wall-clock reduction.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>v3.4.0 (2026-05-16)</strong> — C2PA content-provenance for EU AI Act Article 50 compliance (<code>scripts/embed-c2pa.py</code>, <code>/digital-marketing-pro:c2pa-metadata</code>, pre-publish gate integration). Unified ads-platform MCPs added. Explicit parallel subagent dispatch in <code>engagement-workflow</code> + 4 multi-dimensional commands. Anthropic Software Directory submission packet at <code>SUBMISSION.md</code>.</p>\n<p dir=\"auto\"><strong>v3.3.0 (2026-05-15)</strong> — May 2026 modernization sweep.
